FILE	graphs/Markor/make_verification.py	81683	84ab3f732cca4efa4e5ce5abd98e3062d9d99fd51c5d151775a247437fd89624	utf8
# Writes graphs/Markor/verification.csv (schema 2): path rows for the 30-route sample, label rows for every
# UNREACHABLE method and every reachable sample method, edge rows for the corrected edges and the framework
# citations, and the chain-claim check. Every claim is asserted against the built graph before it is written.
# Route text in each path note is generated from methods.csv and the edge kinds, so it cannot drift from the graph.
# Run from work/:  python3 -I graphs/Markor/make_verification.py   (then build again, so the JSON carries it)
import collections, csv, json, os, re, sys

WORK = '/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang/work/'
G = WORK + 'graphs/Markor/'
SCR = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-darshan-Desktop-yang/0685b904-238e-4df6-a0a5-4168cc5280c3/scratchpad/'
APP = '/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang/final_java_only/Markor/app/Markor-#v2.16.1/app/'
FW = WORK + 'fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1/'
sys.path.insert(0, WORK + 'tools')
import build_graph as b  # has a __main__ guard, so importing runs nothing

g = json.load(open(G + 'out/markor_trigger_graph.json', encoding='utf-8'))
E = {x['id']: x for x in g['edges']}
LAB = g['labels']
rows = {r['method']: r for r in csv.DictReader(open(G + 'out/methods.csv', encoding='utf-8'))}
oldrows = {r['method']: r for r in csv.DictReader(open(SCR + 'methods_before_refs.csv', encoding='utf-8'))}
# the sample is the final verify_sample draw (seed 20261025), read live from the tool; no saved copy is used
import subprocess
_draw = subprocess.run(['python3', '-I', 'tools/verify_sample.py', 'Markor', '30'], cwd=WORK, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
assert _draw.splitlines()[0].startswith('# Markor: 30 of 2450 reachable methods'), _draw.splitlines()[0]  # 2450: 2446 + the four widget service framework nodes (round 7)
SAMPLE = [mm.group(1).strip() for mm in (re.search(r'new edges\s+\d+\s+(.*)$', l) for l in _draw.splitlines()) if mm]
UNRE = {m for m, r in rows.items() if r['verdict'] == 'UNREACHABLE'}
REA = {m for m, r in rows.items() if r['verdict'] == 'REACHABLE'}
assert len(UNRE) == 414 and len(REA) == 2450, (len(UNRE), len(REA))
assert UNRE == {m for m, v in LAB.items() if v.get('unreachable')}
assert len(SAMPLE) == 30 and len(set(SAMPLE)) == 30 and set(SAMPLE) <= REA, SAMPLE


# ---------- helpers: every claim is an assertion on the graph
def between(src, dst):
    return [x for x in g['edges'] if x['src'] == src and x['dst'] == dst]


def has(src, dst, **kw):
    r = [x for x in between(src, dst) if all(v in str(x.get(k) or '') for k, v in kw.items())]
    assert r, ('missing edge', src, dst, kw)
    return r[0]


def absent(src, dst):
    assert not between(src, dst), ('edge present, should be absent', src, dst)


def node_exists(name):
    assert any(x['src'] == name or x['dst'] == name for x in g['edges']), ('no such node', name)


def cb(e):
    return 'SOURCE' if b.edge_sourced(e) else 'INFERRED'


def uref(m):
    # the built JSON keeps an unreachable label's ref and confirmed_by at the top level (kind and text are inside 'unreachable')
    l = LAB[m]
    v = l['source_ref'] if l.get('source_ref') else l['unreachable'].get('ref')
    assert v, m
    return v


def ucb(m):
    l = LAB[m]
    v = l['confirmed_by'] if l.get('confirmed_by') else l['unreachable'].get('confirmed_by')
    assert v in ('SOURCE', 'INFERRED'), (m, v)
    return v


def evref(e):
    return (e.get('evidence') or '')[:160]


def route_ids(m):
    return rows[m]['route_edge_ids'].split()


def chain_ok(m):
    nodes = rows[m]['shortest_path'].split(' > ')
    ids = route_ids(m)
    assert len(ids) == int(rows[m]['steps_from_launch']), (m, len(ids), rows[m]['steps_from_launch'])
    assert len(nodes) == len(ids) + 1, (m, len(nodes), len(ids))
    for i, a, c in zip(ids, nodes, nodes[1:]):
        assert E[i]['src'] == a and E[i]['dst'] == c, (m, i, E[i]['src'], E[i]['dst'], a, c)


def on(m, src, dst):
    assert any(E[i]['src'] == src and E[i]['dst'] == dst for i in route_ids(m)), (m, 'not on route', src, dst)


def route_text(m):
    nodes = rows[m]['shortest_path'].split(' > ')
    out = [nodes[0]]
    for i, n in zip(route_ids(m), nodes[1:]):
        e = E[i]
        tag = e['kind']
        if e.get('env_kinds'):
            tag += ' ENV'
        if e.get('needs_state'):
            tag += ' STATE'
        out.append('-[%s]-> %s' % (tag, n))
    return ' '.join(out)


def sourced_all(m):
    return all(b.edge_sourced(E[i]) for i in route_ids(m))


# ---------- path rows (the 30-route sample): result, hand-written part of the note, and checks
P = {}
COLD = ('Cold start (re-checked this pass): S_ENTRY -> ApplicationObject.<init> -> ApplicationObject.onCreate runs before the '
        'first activity or entry point of this route.')
NB_READING = ('Notebook reading (judgement J_NOTEBOOK): the notebook tab is the '
              'default startup tab (AppSettings.java:781), so after the cold start it is the visible page; this route reaches it from '
              'MainActivity.onCreate without an onResume step.')


def p(m, res, text, checks=()):
    assert m in SAMPLE, m
    chain_ok(m)
    for c in checks:
        if c[0] == 'on':
            on(m, c[1], c[2])
        elif c[0] == 'has':
            has(c[1], c[2], **(c[3] if len(c) > 3 else {}))
        elif c[0] == 'absent':
            absent(c[1], c[2])
        elif c[0] == 'node':
            node_exists(c[1])
        elif c[0] == 'onenv':
            # a route edge whose environment text contains c[3]
            assert any(E[i]['src'] == c[1] and E[i]['dst'] == c[2] and c[3] in str(E[i].get('env') or '') for i in route_ids(m)), (m, 'no such env edge on route', c)
        elif c[0] == 'stated':
            # every edge between two nodes carries a state gate containing c[3]
            r = between(c[1], c[2])
            assert r and all(c[3] in str(x.get('needs_state') or '') for x in r), (c, [x['id'] for x in r])
        elif c[0] == 'guardon':
            # the built edge from c[1] to c[2] carries a guard containing c[3], and that edge is on this route
            r = [x for x in between(c[1], c[2]) if c[3] in str(x.get('guard') or '')]
            assert r, ('guard missing on edge', c)
            assert any(x['id'] in route_ids(m) for x in r), ('guarded edge not on route', m, c)
        elif c[0] == 'into_only':
            # the only edges into c[1] come from c[2]
            srcs = {x['src'] for x in g['edges'] if x['dst'] == c[1]}
            assert srcs == {c[2]}, (c, srcs)
        else:
            raise ValueError(c[0])
    note = '%s %s Route (%s steps): %s' % (text, COLD, rows[m]['steps_from_launch'], route_text(m))
    P[m] = {'res': res, 'note': note, 'cb': 'SOURCE' if sourced_all(m) else 'INFERRED'}

VPS = 'MainActivity.onViewPagerPageSelected'

assert len(P) == 30 and set(P) == set(SAMPLE), set(SAMPLE) ^ set(P)

# ---- cold-start check on every sampled route: S_ENTRY -> ApplicationObject.<init> -> ApplicationObject.onCreate come first,
# every activity constructor is followed by its onCreate, and no lifecycle step is skipped (skip counts must be 0).
# Judgement tags are counted, not failures. J_RESUME is asserted to be exactly the two routes that pass
# MainActivity.onResume without MainActivity.onPostResume.
def mname(n):
    return n.split('(')[0].split('@')[0].rsplit('.', 1)[-1]


def mcls(n):
    return n.split('(')[0].split('@')[0].rsplit('.', 1)[0]


SKIP = {'<init>': ('onResume', 'onPause', 'onPostResume', 'onActivityFirstTimeVisible'),
        'onCreate': ('onPause', 'onPostResume', 'onActivityFirstTimeVisible'),
        'onResume': ('onPause', 'onActivityFirstTimeVisible')}
LIFE = ('<init>', 'onCreate', 'onResume', 'onPostResume', 'onPause')
LIFE_NEXT = {('<init>', 'onCreate'), ('onCreate', 'onResume'), ('onResume', 'onPostResume'), ('onPostResume', 'onPause')}
ACT_EXTRA = {'WrWidgetConfigure'}  # an activity without 'Activity' in its name (has LIFECYCLE <init> -> onCreate)
cold_rows = [['item', 'result', 'start_ok', 'ctor_ok', 'skip_hits', 'tags', 'first_steps']]
cold_tags = {}
for m in SAMPLE:
    nodes = rows[m]['shortest_path'].split(' > ')
    names = [mname(n) for n in nodes]
    start_ok = nodes[:3] == ['S_ENTRY', 'ApplicationObject.<init>', 'ApplicationObject.onCreate']
    ctor_ok = True
    skip_hits = 0
    for i, n in enumerate(nodes[:-1]):
        a, c = names[i], names[i + 1]
        is_act = ('Activity' in mcls(n) or mcls(n) in ACT_EXTRA) and 'Fragment' not in mcls(n)
        # the skip rules are about activity lifecycles only (a fragment's onResume -> onPause is ordinary)
        if is_act and a in SKIP and c in SKIP[a]:
            skip_hits += 1
        if a == '<init>' and is_act and c != 'onCreate':
            ctor_ok = False
    # MainActivity lifecycle order: each lifecycle step may follow only the step before it
    life = [mname(n) for n in nodes if mcls(n) == 'MainActivity' and mname(n) in LIFE]
    for prev_step, next_step in zip(life, life[1:]):
        if (prev_step, next_step) not in LIFE_NEXT:
            skip_hits += 1
    on_route = set(nodes)
    tags = []
    if 'MainActivity.onResume' in on_route and 'MainActivity.onPostResume' not in on_route:
        tags.append('J_RESUME')
    if 'MainActivity.onCreate' in on_route and 'MainActivity.onResume' not in on_route:
        tags.append('J_NOTEBOOK')
    if 'DocumentActivity.onCreate@SEND' in on_route and 'DocumentActivity.onResume' not in on_route:
        tags.append('J_SHARE')
    assert start_ok, (m, nodes[:3])
    assert ctor_ok, (m, 'activity constructor not followed by onCreate')
    assert skip_hits == 0, (m, 'skipped lifecycle step', skip_hits)
    cold_tags[m] = tags
    if 'J_NOTEBOOK' in tags and 'AppSettings.java:781' not in P[m]['note']:
        # the same notebook reading as the SearchAndReplaceTextDialog route: onCreate -> notebook, no onResume on this route
        P[m]['note'] = P[m]['note'].replace(' Route (', ' ' + NB_READING + ' Route (', 1)
    cold_rows.append([m, P[m]['res'], start_ok, ctor_ok, skip_hits, ','.join(tags) or '-', ' > '.join(nodes[:4])])
# J_RESUME: the routes that pass MainActivity.onResume without onPostResume. isEncryptedFile now enters the notebook from onPostResume (fix 1), so only one route is left.
assert sorted(m for m in SAMPLE if 'J_RESUME' in cold_tags[m]) == ['StoragePermissionActivity.processPermissionState'], cold_tags
with open(SCR + 'cold_start_table.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    for r in cold_rows:
        f.write('\t'.join(str(x) for x in r) + '\n')
print('cold start: routes', len(SAMPLE), 'start_ok', sum(r[2] is True for r in cold_rows[1:]),
      'ctor_ok', sum(r[3] is True for r in cold_rows[1:]), 'skip_hits', sum(r[4] for r in cold_rows[1:]))
for t in ('J_RESUME', 'J_NOTEBOOK', 'J_SHARE'):
    print('  tag', t, sum(1 for m in SAMPLE if t in cold_tags[m]))

res_counts = collections.Counter(v['res'] for v in P.values())
assert res_counts == {'correct': 13, 'fixed': 17}, res_counts  # round 8: getLoadingView, getCount and compareTo replace getItemId, getViewTypeCount and clearAdditional; search$0 is now fixed (its guards are on the graph)
assert res_counts.get('wrong', 0) == 0, res_counts

out = [['type', 'item', 'result', 'confirmed_by', 'ref', 'edges', 'note']]
for m in SAMPLE:
    v = P[m]
    out.append(['path', m, v['res'], v['cb'], LAB[m]['source_ref'], rows[m]['route_edge_ids'].strip(), v['note']])

assert oa != uref('StoragePermissionActivity.onActivityResult')

    'FIXED (S3). The first graph had a FRAMEWORK edge from StoragePermissionActivity.<init> to this hook; the hook runs only after startActivityForResult, '

    '(SOURCE): the app\'s only call (line 320) passes a Preference and binds to the Preference overload.' % ok_old)

ref_fix = json.load(open(G + 'scratch/ref_fix.json', encoding='utf-8'))
assert len(ref_fix) == 47, len(ref_fix)


def simple_name(key):
    cls, _, rest = key.partition('.')
    if rest.startswith('<init>'):
        return re.split(r'[$.]', cls)[-1]
    if rest.startswith('lambda'):
        return None
    return rest.split('(')[0]


def name_on_line(key, ref):
    mm = re.match(r'(.+?):(\d+)', ref)
    if not mm:
        return 'noref'
    path, line = mm.group(1), int(mm.group(2))
    if not os.path.isfile(APP + path):
        return 'nofile'
    text = open(APP + path, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().splitlines()
    name = simple_name(key)
    if name is None:
        return 'lambda'
    return str(name in '\n'.join(text[max(0, line - 3):line + 2]))


name_check = collections.Counter()
for k, new in ref_fix.items():
    assert k in UNRE, k
    old = oldrows[k]['source_ref']
    assert old != new, (k, old)
    assert uref(k) == new, (k, uref(k), new)
    name_check[name_on_line(k, new)] += 1
print('ref_fix name check on the corrected line:', dict(name_check))
assert name_check['False'] == 0, 'a corrected ref does not show the method name'

chain_total = 0
for m in sorted(UNRE):
    u = dict(LAB[m]['unreachable'], ref=uref(m), confirmed_by=ucb(m))
    assert rows[m]['unreachable_kind'] == u['kind'], (m, rows[m]['unreachable_kind'], u['kind'])
    assert rows[m]['unreachable_reason'] == u['text'], m
    assert rows[m]['source_ref'] == u['ref'] and rows[m]['confirmed_by'] == u['confirmed_by'], (m, rows[m]['source_ref'], u['ref'])
    cbv = u['confirmed_by']
    if m in FIXED_UN:
        out.append(['label', m, 'fixed', cbv, u['ref'], '', FIXED_UN[m] + ' Kind %s.' % u['kind']])
        continue
    tail = ''
    mm = re.search(r'every caller is unreachable \((.*)\)$', u['text'])
    if mm:
        callers = mm.group(1).split(', ')
        for c in callers:
            assert c in UNRE, ('chain claim names a caller that is not UNREACHABLE', m, c)
        chain_total += 1
        tail = ' Chain check: caller(s) %s are UNREACHABLE in the final labels (asserted).' % ', '.join(callers)
    elif cbv == 'INFERRED':
        tail = ' INFERRED: the reason depends on a library class that is not on disk.'
    if m in ref_fix:
        old = oldrows[m]['source_ref']
        out.append(['label', m, 'fixed', cbv, u['ref'], '',
                    'FIXED (overload citation). The entry cited %s (the first or a same-arity overload); corrected to %s. Kind %s. %s%s'
                    % (old, u['ref'], u['kind'], u['text'], tail)])
    else:
        out.append(['label', m, 'correct', cbv, u['ref'], '',
                    'Kind %s, reason and ref as stored in the graph (asserted equal to methods.csv). %s%s' % (u['kind'], u['text'], tail)])
assert chain_total == 72, chain_total

# ---- label rows: every reachable sample method
for m in SAMPLE:
    v = P[m]
    l = LAB[m]
    if v['res'] in ('correct', 'fixed'):
        lres = 'correct'
        note = 'REACHABLE. Confirmed by its route (path row, result %s).' % v['res']
    else:
        lres = 'unclear'
        note = 'REACHABLE, but the route in the path row is wrong, so the label is not confirmed by it (see the path row).'
    out.append(['label', m, lres, l['confirmed_by'], l['source_ref'], '', note])

# ---- edge rows
# tools/build_graph.py reads an 'edge' row's item column as the edge id and warns if that id is not in the graph,
# so every edge row carries the real id in item and edges. A removed (suppressed) edge has no id: its row is not written
# (a warning would be wrong), and the removal is asserted above and described in the path notes and readme_notes.md.
skipped_edge_rows = []


def erow(desc, edge_id, res, conf, ref, note):
    if not edge_id:
        skipped_edge_rows.append(desc)
        return
    assert edge_id in E, edge_id
    out.append(['edge', edge_id, res, conf, ref, edge_id, '%s. %s' % (desc, note)])

## What the re-audit changed (compared with the schema 1 graph)

- Markor 2.16.1 is now schema 2: 2864 methods (2859 in method_list.json and five framework nodes: IntroActivity.onResume, and onCreate and onBind of WrFilesWidgetService and TodoWidgetService), 19 pages, 6891 edges. Of the methods, 2450 are REACHABLE and 414 UNREACHABLE (404 no_trigger_in_app, 9 closed_by_configuration, 1 closed_by_api_level). The unreachable labels are confirmed by SOURCE (272) or INFERRED (142).
- Tabs: the todo, quick note and more fragments are created only when their tab is realized (MainActivity.onViewPagerPageSelected, via ensureRealized at MainActivity.java:446 and 548-553). The base edges that created them on every page change are absent. The More page has its own receiver variant, MainActivity.onViewPagerPageSelected@MORE, whose framework edge goes to MoreFragment.onViewCreated (MoreFragment.java:46).
- Receiver lifts: the base handler GsPreferenceFragmentBase.onPreferenceTreeClick is no longer lifted to subclass handlers, and the lift that reached PasswordStore.createSecretKey is removed (_LIFT_SKIP in graph_def.py). The share-into page keeps its own GUI edge (S_SHARE_INTO).
- Widgets: the shared widget state is split into S_HOME_WIDGET_NOTEBOOK and S_HOME_WIDGET_TODO. The todo widget's intent targets OpenFromShortcutOrWidgetActivity (TodoWidgetProvider.java:38-42).
- Editor menu: the editor menu Runnable starts MainActivity with the file (DocumentEditAndViewFragment.java:630). It is the route used for MainActivity.onNewIntent (lambda$onOptionsItemSelected$9).
- Result handler: StoragePermissionActivity.onActivityResult is closed by API level. On API 27, GsContextUtils.requestExternalStoragePermission calls only ActivityCompat.requestPermissions (GsContextUtils.java:2387-2401). startActivityForResult is used only at SDK 30 and above (2389, 2393, 2397). The framework wiring from StoragePermissionActivity.<init> to onActivityResult was removed.
- Syntax highlighting: the edges from SyntaxHighlighterBase.compute to the Asciidoc and key-value generators carry the format state in needs_state, so they run only when that format is set.
- Share intents: the only edge into DocumentActivity.showShareInto comes from DocumentActivity.handleLaunchingIntent@SEND, with env "another app sends the intent" (DocumentActivity.java:172-178). The base edge from handleLaunchingIntent is absent.
- Base-class handlers: base edges that reached subclass handlers from every page (for example toolbar and key handlers) were removed where they gave wrong routes. Each owning page now has its own edge. Two of these base edges stay ungated (see Hazard candidates).
- Citations: 47 overload citations were corrected (graphs/Markor/scratch/ref_fix.json; the rebuild's name check passes for all 47). 16 bytecode citations point at the constructor call line (_BC_LINE_FIX), not the start of the statement. One try-with-resources close is labelled implicit (_BC_TEXT_FIX; CsvTextConverter.java:88).
- Build flags: BuildConfig.IS_TEST_BUILD is true, so isSpecialFileLaunchersEnabled() returns false (AppSettings.java:254-258). The main manifest has android:enabled="false" at lines 141, 158 and 171.
- Cold start and lifecycle (this pass):
  - The notebook entry is now ENTERS from MainActivity.onPostResume (it was from MainActivity.onCreate). The default startup tab is the notebook (AppSettings.java:781).
  - The direct MainActivity.<init> -> MainActivity.onResume edge is removed. The count of direct `<init> -> onResume` edges from an activity is 0 for MainActivity. The four `<init> -> onResume` edges in the graph are all FRAMEWORK edges to GsPreferenceFragmentBase.onResume from preference fragments (DocumentShareIntoFragment$ShareIntoImportOptionsFragment, MoreInfoFragment, SettingsActivity$MarkorSettingsFragment, SettingsActivity$SettingsFragmentMaster); none is from an activity.
  - ENTERS edges that started at onCreate moved to the resumed step: DocumentActivity (onResume@EDIT -> S_DOCUMENT; onResume@SHARE -> S_SHARE_INTO), WrWidgetConfigure (onResume@WRCONFIG -> S_WIDGET_CONFIG), and the ActionButtonSettings and Settings activities (onResume@ACTIONBTN and @SETTINGS). The intro is the framework case: its ENTERS to S_INTRO now starts from the framework node IntroActivity.onResume (FRAMEWORK_NODES in graph_def.py), reached from IntroActivity.onCreate by a LIFECYCLE edge (see Limits).
  - Toolbar: GsActivityBase.onResume -> GsActivityBase.onToolbarClick is a registration that route search skips (_REG_SKIP). The tap is a GUI edge from S_NOTEBOOK to GsActivityBase.onToolbarClick, guarded by the toolbar listener (toolbar != null) (GsActivityBase.java:94-98, 116-124).
  - Widget services: Service.onCreate and RemoteViewsService.onBind are framework nodes (FRAMEWORK_NODES in graph_def.py: onCreate and onBind of TodoWidgetService and WrFilesWidgetService). The LIFECYCLE chains run <init> -> onCreate -> onBind -> onGetViewFactory, cited to AOSP android-8.1.0_r1 (see Limits).
  - scrollToAndFlash: the FRAMEWORK edge from MainActivity.onCreate to MainActivity.onClickFab (E68c24288) is gone. The FAB tap is now a GUI edge from S_NOTEBOOK (E390e1c1e).

## How the checked paths were chosen

- The 30 path rows are the final draw of `tools/verify_sample.py Markor 30` (seed 20261025). make_verification.py runs that draw itself at each rebuild, so the path rows follow the draw. The draw has six strata with these counts: NONSTD 4, INFERRED 4, STATE 3, ENV 6, LONG 6 (12 or more steps), PLAIN 7. Inside a stratum, the method that adds the most edges not yet covered comes first.
- The logged path rows equal the final draw: 30 of 30, as a set and in the same order. The draw is identical on re-draw (checked after the last rebuild, 2026-10-27).
- Result for the 30 path rows: 13 correct, 17 fixed, 0 wrong (17/30 = 56.7%). A fixed route is one whose built edges lacked a condition the code requires (guard, receiver check, listener guard, or cold-start step); each path row's note says which.
- Cold start, checked on all 30 routes (the cold-start block in make_verification.py):
  - Graph basis: MainActivity.<init> -> MainActivity.onCreate -> MainActivity.onResume -> MainActivity.onPostResume -> MainActivity.onPause (graph_def.py:125-134). Framework order: ActivityThread.java:2729 (callActivityOnCreate), 2741 (performStart), 2950 (performResume); Activity.java:7107 (onResume) and 7131 (onPostResume).
  - All 30 start S_ENTRY -> ApplicationObject.<init> -> ApplicationObject.onCreate. All 30 have each activity constructor followed by its onCreate. Skip hits: 0. The skip rules apply to activity classes only; a fragment's onResume -> onPause is ordinary.
  - First-start intro (not sampled; show_route, 11 steps): MainActivity.onResume -> IntroActivity.optStart -> IntroActivity.<init> -> IntroActivity.onCreate -> IntroActivity.onResume -> S_INTRO -> IntroActivity.onNextPressed (the tap). The state gate is 'the intro flag is not set' (first start only).
  - Judgement tags are counted, not failures. Each route's note states its reading:
    - J_RESUME (1): StoragePermissionActivity.processPermissionState. The route passes MainActivity.onResume without MainActivity.onPostResume. The notebook is the default startup tab (AppSettings.java:781), and MainActivity.onActivityFirstTimeVisible (MainActivity.java:142-144) switches tabs only when the startup tab is not the notebook, so after the cold start the notebook is the visible, resumed page. No S_NOTEBOOK edge is modelled as missing.
    - J_NOTEBOOK (1): GsSharedPreferencesPropertyBackend.getStringListOne. The route reaches the notebook from MainActivity.onCreate with no onResume step. The notebook is the default startup tab (AppSettings.java:781), so after the cold start it is the visible page. The note says so.
    - J_SHARE (2): DocumentShareIntoFragment$ShareIntoImportOptionsFragment.getLinkTitle and lambda$hasLinks$1. These enter DocumentActivity from another app (SEND, environment) with no DocumentActivity.onResume step. Judgement: the share intent is an external entry. This tag is counted in the cold-start block only; the CSV notes do not carry it.
    - J_SERVICE was removed from the cold-start block. The widget factory routes run through the RemoteViewsService constructor and onGetViewFactory, and the framework steps are trigger text (see Limits).
- Changed since the stale copy (verification_stale.csv, the pre-pass copy): 9 routes left the draw and 9 entered it; 21 of the 30 current routes are in both copies.
  - Left the draw, not re-verified in this pass (prior result correct each): GsContextUtils.isDeviceGoodHardware, AppSettings.<clinit>, GsSharedPreferencesPropertyBackend.<clinit>, SyntaxHighlighterBase$SpanGroup.compareTo.
  - Removed from the path rows in make_verification.py in this pass (prior result fixed each in the script before removal): PasswordStore.createSecretKey, FileSearchResultSelectorDialog$ExpandableSearchResultsListAdapter.getGroup, DocumentShareIntoFragment$ShareIntoImportOptionsFragment$1.lambda$onFsViewerSelected$0, AttachLinkOrFileDialog.lambda$fetchAndInsertItem$9, and SearchAndReplaceTextDialog$ReplaceGroup.toJson (the stale copy has this one as correct, because its route changed in the cold-start fix).
  - Entered the draw (result correct each): WrWidgetConfigure$1.<init>, GsSharedPreferencesPropertyBackend.gp, GsContextUtils.<init>, GsContextUtils.setChooserTitle; and, added in this pass, ActionButtonSettingsActivity.lambda$saveNewOrder$0, DocumentEditAndViewFragment$2.lambda$search$0, AppSettings.getAttachmentFolder, GsFileBrowserListAdapter.createDirectoryHere, GsSharedPreferencesPropertyBackend.getStringListOne.
  - Result changes between the two copies: correct -> fixed for WrFilesWidgetFactory.getCount and TodoWidgetRemoteViewsFactory.getViewTypeCount (the cold start now runs through onCreate). No other result changed.
- Round 6 (IntroActivity.onResume node): the sample changed by one route. Removed from the path rows (prior result fixed): WrFilesWidgetFactory.getCount, whose route ends at WrFilesWidgetFactory.onDataSetChanged -> getCount. Added (result correct): WrFilesWidgetFactory.getItemId, which returns the position (WrFilesWidgetFactory.java:105-107), so it has no condition of its own; its route is the same cold-start widget route. Two routes changed strata but stayed in the draw (GsFileBrowserListAdapter.createDirectoryHere and FlexmarkKatexExtension$KatexInlineMath.getText).
- The FAB note on lambda$scrollToAndFlash$3 was corrected in this pass. Its route goes through the notebook folder tap (S_NOTEBOOK -> GsFileBrowserListAdapter.onClick), not through the FAB. The FAB edges are checked in their own rows.
- Edge rows: 87 (42 correct, 40 fixed, 5 unclear). ## Hazard candidates (from reading the code, not reproduced)

These are candidate misroutes found in the source. Each is a base-class method that reaches a handler with no page check. None was run on a device.

1. Notebook toolbar: GsActivityBase.onToolbarClick (GsActivityBase.java:116-124) calls onToolbarClicked on the first visible, resumed GsFragmentBase, so it is not sent to every page. The edge to GsFileBrowserFragment.onToolbarClicked (GsActivityBase.java:120) carries that loop as a guard and a receiver check (the notebook fragment). The drawn route through it (FileSearchEngine.isEncryptedFile) runs S_NOTEBOOK before the toolbar tap, so the notebook page must be on screen at the tap; that is a stated assumption.
2. Key press: MarkorBaseActivity.onReceiveKeyPress calls GsFragmentBase.onReceiveKeyPress (MarkorBaseActivity.java:36; CALL, bytecode, no state or env). No drawn route uses it (checked on the shortest paths).
3. Notebook list: the INFERRED FRAMEWORK edges from GsFileBrowserFragment.onViewCreated to the notebook list adapter (for example accept(File) and getItemId) have no bytecode caller in the graph. Their callbacks are not read per listener.
4. Shortcut: MainActivity.onPostResume to onActivityFirstTimeVisible is one FRAMEWORK edge (Edb90cf71). It stands for super.onPostResume (MainActivity.java:318) and the Handler post in GsActivityBase.onPostResume (GsActivityBase.java:106). It is sourced, so it is not a known misroute. It is kept and unclear.
5. FAB click: the plus button's tap (S_NOTEBOOK -> MainActivity.onClickFab, E390e1c1e) is INFERRED, because the listener type is not read per listener. The registration at MainActivity.java:91 is recorded but is not a route step. No drawn route passes the tap now.

## Limits

- onStart is not modelled. No page in the Markor app overrides onStart; the only onStart override in the app tree is GsFileBrowserDialog.java:343 (a dialog, in opoc/frontend/filebrowser). The getStringListOne route (MainActivity.onCreate -> createFragment -> GsFileBrowserFragment.onViewCreated) has no onStart and no onResume step, and no tap. Its fragment views are stated to be created during the start, before onResume: onCreate calls mFragments.dispatchCreate (FW-SRC core/java/android/app/Activity.java:1014), and performStart dispatches the fragment start at 7025 (7014-7025, before performResume at 7098). Whether the androidx pager creates the view in onCreate or in the dispatchStart step is INFERRED (androidx FragmentManager and the pager source are not on disk). The route is kept on this limit.
- Menu order: the notebook toolbar menu route (FlexmarkKatexExtension$KatexInlineMath.getText, "Tap an item of the notebook toolbar menu") is modelled after the resume step: MainActivity.onResume -> MainActivity.onPostResume -> S_NOTEBOOK -> menu. The AOSP lines read are Activity.java:3386-3389 (onCreatePanelMenu builds the options menu), FragmentManager.java:3108 (dispatchCreateOptionsMenu) and Activity.java:7098-7131 (performResume: onResume at 7107, dispatchResume at 7128-7129, onPostResume at 7131). These lines do not show when the options menu is built relative to onResume, so the order is a modelling choice, not a pinned fact.
- Widget service lifecycle (round 8, fixed): Service.onCreate (core/java/android/app/Service.java:343) and RemoteViewsService.onBind and onGetViewFactory (core/java/android/widget/RemoteViewsService.java:235-244, 257) are now framework nodes (FRAMEWORK_NODES in graph_def.py: onCreate and onBind of WrFilesWidgetService and TodoWidgetService). The app's service classes declare only <init> and onGetViewFactory, so the inherited methods are attached to them. LIFECYCLE edges run <init> -> onCreate -> onBind -> onGetViewFactory. build_graph writes the node records from FRAMEWORK_NODES (they are not in method_list.json).
- IntroActivity (modelled in round 6): ENTERS S_INTRO now leaves from the framework node IntroActivity.onResume (FRAMEWORK_NODES in graph_def.py). IntroActivity does not override onResume, so the call is the inherited Activity.onResume (FW-SRC core/java/android/app/Activity.java:7098-7131, the call at 7107; performCreate calls onCreate at 6989-6999). The LIFECYCLE edge IntroActivity.onCreate -> IntroActivity.onResume carries the same citations. The node is not in method_list.json, so its record is written by build_graph from FRAMEWORK_NODES. AppIntro, the base class, is a library not on disk, so its hooks (onNextPressed and the others) stay INFERRED.
- The notebook fragment view is created from onCreate through the pager (MainActivity.java:445-446, 519-522). Its timing is not run.
- Copy and info selection guards. Copy (GsFileBrowserFragment.java:439-440): the source calls askForMoveOrCopy with no selection check, so the graph has no guard and none is missing. Info (GsFileBrowserFragment.java:457-460, fixed in round 8): the source checks _filesystemViewerAdapter.areItemsSelected() (line 458) before FileInfoDialog.show. The graph has that guard on the GUI edge S_NOTEBOOK_SEL -> FileInfoDialog.show (needs NEED_SELECTED). The notebook row long-press enters S_NOTEBOOK_SEL (guard: view.getId() == R.id.opoc_filesystem_item__root). The menu's own FileInfoDialog.show edge from GsFileBrowserFragment.onOptionsItemSelected is suppressed, so the notebook Info route cannot skip the selection check. The todo editor's Info edge carries the guard saveDocument(false) returns true (DocumentEditAndViewFragment.java:605-606; route_guards.json key with line 606).
- Search guards (DocumentEditAndViewFragment.java:707-708, fixed in round 8): search returns false when _webView is null, before the debounced findAllAsync (line 711). The graph now has the check on two edges: setupSearchView -> DocumentEditAndViewFragment$2.onQueryTextChange (guard: not (!_isPreviewVisible || _webView == null); needs NEED_PREVIEW, 'The editor is in preview mode (the view mode was chosen from the menu or the swipe gesture)') and DocumentEditAndViewFragment$2.search -> lambda$search$0 (guard: not (_webView == null)). The preview-mode state is a setup state that no route produces.
- Row listeners (cited in round 8): the row click listeners are set in GsFileBrowserListAdapter (lines 271, 426, 440). The notebook row tap (S_NOTEBOOK -> GsFileBrowserListAdapter.onClick) cites line 271 and the view framework (FW-SRC core/java/android/view/View.java:6289-6296, performClick). The edge GsFileBrowserListAdapter.onClick -> MainActivity$2.onFsViewerSelected cites 426 (the single-file row tap) and 440 (the OK button), both calls to the same dialog listener. Dialog row taps are not modelled as their own step (accepted limit): the graph draws no edge from the dialog's row views to the adapter, and no drawn route depends on a dialog row tap.
- _REG_SKIP: the toolbar registration (GsActivityBase.java:94-98) is skipped by route search, so the GUI edge from S_NOTEBOOK carries the tap.
- Gate items (counted before this pass's last edits; not re-counted): 14 unique items. Six are for-each loops over orderedKeys or appWidgetIds (a loop is not a condition). Three are label lookups that picked the wrong case label (pref_key__more_info__app, AUDIO_RECORDING, ui__filesystem_dialog__home; the built guards cite the right ones). One is a dropped '!' in a conjunction (forceOpenInThisApp, built correctly). One is the OutOfMemoryError catch in updateViewModeText (the call runs on both paths). One is an equivalent phrasing (attachment == null, same as not (attachment != null)). Two are the PROCESS_TEXT alternative of the shared SEND edge (E73fb7cb9, DocumentActivity.java:177); the SEND route takes the intentIsSend alternative.
- The format-menu route (SyntaxHighlighterBase$1.draw) depends on the user picking a format in the editor's format menu. No route sets a format state by itself. NEED_FIX is a setup state, not one a route produces.
- setComputed clears the highlighter's fixup on every full recompute (SyntaxHighlighterBase.java:418-425). The clearFixup route left the final draw, so this is not checked on a sampled route now.
- Page-format exclusivity (one format per open document) is enforced only by the state labels, not by a check in the code path.
- Widget providers: TodoWidgetProvider.<init> and WrMarkorWidgetProvider.<init> are noted as ENV-gated, but they run in the graph, and the sanity check flags them (2 ENV-gated on the best route but ran). Open.
- Method references are not checked per target type: the FAB click (MainActivity.java:91) and the new-item callbacks (MainActivity.java:195 and 359) are wired from the call site.
- Citation items still open (not used in a final route): GsContextUtils 1110-1140 beyond 1116-1117; preferences_master.xml around line 210; GsFontPreferenceCompat 60-120 and 235-250; AndroidBug5497Workaround 20-45.

Hazard 1 (toolbar) has the loop guard and the receiver check; its route runs S_NOTEBOOK before the toolbar tap, so the on-screen page is a stated assumption. Other base-class handlers were checked only where they lie on the 30 sampled routes. The other 2415 reachable methods were not checked, so a correct sampled route says nothing about them.

# Markor schema 2: lessons from errors that were found

- Route checks found more errors than label checks. Before the route-fix pass, 14 of the 30 sampled routes were wrong. A label can be right while the path to it is wrong, so check paths too.
- A lift from a base-class method to subclass overrides needs proof that the subclass runs there. Two lifts gave wrong routes (GsPreferenceFragmentBase.onPreferenceTreeClick, PasswordStore.createSecretKey); both are skipped now (_LIFT_SKIP).
- Do not share one state node between components that differ. The two home-screen widgets shared S_HOME_WIDGET, so one widget's route reached the other's edges. It is split into S_HOME_WIDGET_NOTEBOOK and S_HOME_WIDGET_TODO.
- Base-class calls carry no page check, so a base handler reached subclass handlers from every page. Each owning page has its own edge now. A ViewPager tab runs its branch only when the tab is realized, so the base tab edges were removed and the More page has its own variant.
- Cite the line that holds the call. Overload citations pointed at another overload's line (47 fixed), and 16 bytecode citations pointed at the start of a statement instead of the constructor call.
- The logged sample must be the draw that is checked. After the route fixes, 11 of the 30 logged routes were not the current draw. make_verification.py now runs the draw itself at each rebuild and fails if the logged set differs.
- Read the source line before adding a guard, and where a base handler picks its target at run time, the edge needs a receiver check (GsActivityBase.onToolbarClick). The bytecode gate gave false misses: a switch label from the wrong case, a dropped "!", and a shared disjunction branch.
- A cold-start route must run an activity's lifecycle in order: constructor, onCreate, onResume, onPostResume. Five routes went MainActivity.<init> -> onResume and were removed. A tap is reached from the page that shows it, and a REGISTER edge is not a route step. An entry from another app (SEND intent) is not a route from the in-app launch.

- Edge wrapper e() records hand edges in _HAND. C6 loop: one FRAMEWORK edge from each bytecode lambda registrar to its body (evidence INFERRED: the listener type is not read per lambda). Builder dedupes REGISTER edges against these hand FRAMEWORK edges (build 5 edge count 6032, checked in out/build_report.txt).
- Citation fixes this session: removed a SAF result edge from DocumentActivity (no caller exists); the SAF caller is GsContextUtils.showMountSdDialog (lambda at 2271); MainActivity.java 391-393 was not verified and was removed; several line ranges narrowed to verified lines.
- Build 5: methods 2859, pages 16, edges 6032, REACHABLE 1694, UNRESOLVED 1165, errors 1165 (all "no path and no UNREACHABLE entry"), warnings 0. UNREACHABLE: none written yet.
- Unresolved split (build 5): no bytecode caller (non-lambda) 552 + 73 init; caller exists but unreached 391 + 56 init; lambdas 91 whose registrar is unreached; 2 clinit.
- Dead-code finding: AppSettings.getRating, getLastEditScrollY, getFileBrowserLastBrowsedFolder, isEditorStartOnBotttom have no call site in src/main (grep). Candidates for no_trigger_in_app after a reference check.
- Next: per class, decide wire (framework callback, layout inflation, listener, adapter, dialog) or UNREACHABLE (no reference in src/main, confirmed by grep), then build until only true UNREACHABLE remain.

## Status (2026-10-27, section E: reachability-driven callbacks)
- Builds 6-7: the evidence prefix must be "INFERRED " (no colon); build_graph counted "INFERRED:" separately.
- Section D1 (static `_INC` skip) replaced by section E in graph_def.py: a search from S_ENTRY (same rule as the builder: no REGISTER edges) runs inside graph_def; each framework or library callback (name in _CB_NAMES, class with an external supertype or an app override) is wired from the bytecode creators of the class and its non-overriding subclasses, only once a creator is reached (fixpoint, <= 60 passes). Menu placeholder methods are wired only when the app text calls the name with a dot (scratch/dotcount.json).
- Build 11 figures: REACHABLE 2166, UNRESOLVED 693, edges 6287, warnings 0. The graph_def run raised NameError twice (missing _CREATORS, then candidate node names from method_list 'short' lack the parameter list for overloads: use AUTO['impl'][simple][name][desc]). LESSON: check for "Traceback" in the build output before reading out/ files, because out/build_report.txt from an earlier build stays on disk.
- Roots now (scratch/status.py): 407 with no non-REGISTER incoming edge (plain 138, ext_ancestor 133, init 73, static 48, private 8, app_override 6, clinit 1); 252 of them have no identifier occurrence in app text beyond declarations; 155 have some.
- Tool gap found: method references (`this::name`, `Class::name`, 29 app files) are not in AUTO['lambdas'] (only lambda$ bodies are), so their targets get no REGISTER edge. To handle with FRAMEWORK edges from the enclosing method (INFERRED), listed by grep.
- Dead chains: SUPPRESS in the builder drops an auto edge when (src, dst) is in the set; to keep the "UNREACHABLE but has incoming edges" warning meaningful, suppress only (src, dst) pairs whose src is UNREACHABLE (not bare names: a bare dst name would hide live callers of a wrongly dead method).
- Still to do: decide the 155 roots with references (interface dispatch of GsPropertyBackend implementations, XML-inflated views and preferences, action-button lists, Fragment and Preference callbacks, method references); then mark the textually dead roots UNREACHABLE (kind, reason, declaration ref, confirmed_by SOURCE); then closure for chains; then verification steps.
